The JSON's slot paths are unchanged (`slotN` already won); the feature keys follow JR8.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-18)
- **JR14 Export reads what the JSON carries (final review C1, C2, I1, I2, I4; controller rulings 3b-F2 and 3b-F3, 2026-11-18).** Spec E1: a slot filled by a path from another set "is a filled slot everywhere", Export included. With no link and no clear, Export's blocks and written JSON are what they were (`partial_export::with_no_link_and_no_clear_export_blocks_and_writes_what_it_did`).
  - **Path identity and the index.** A link is looked up with PGPatcher's own explicit-slot rule (`pgpsim` `slots.rs`, the `slot1`..`slot8` pass): ASCII lower case, `textures\` put in front unless the string starts with it (`txst_map::slot_path_key`), so `pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` and `textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` are one file and `other\textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` is not that file. Note HG's record paths keep `game_key`. The index (`txst_map::written_outputs`) holds every planned output of every set (each member that writes a file of its own: the members after the first of a slot kind and the outputs that fill no slot included), built in one pass with each output's kind (`slot_of`) and its set's short name; so every pick the From another set menu offers is a link to its set and logs its Info line. An output that fills no slot (a complex `_m`) is a local file with no kind, so it counts for no feature (JR8 needs a kind).
  - **One answer to "does this set write an entry".** `pbr_export::writes_entry` is `exports_json` (CS, a JSON set, a match that applies) and the statics rule keeping the entry (`pgp_entry_wanted`; a CS set with statics set to Never writes none). The link display, the feature defaults (`features`), the missing-map suppression and the cleared state read it through `link_slots`, and `written_json_entries` filters by it. On a set that writes no entry a stored link or clear changes no feature default and drops no missing-map row, and an own file under `slotN = ""` shows as itself (no dim chip, no "Left out of the JSON" sentence): `slot_shown`'s `Cleared` needs the file left out of `features`. `link_slots` asks `writes_entry` only of a set with a `slotN` string.
  - **Validation.** Export's blocks read what the JSON names (`sets::json_files`, over the fields the feature keys read: `feature_slots` and `linked`), in three states: a local file (an own or borrowed fill no `slotN` overrides, or a link that names a set's output, on the row it fills), a typed path no set writes (it fills its game slot for the JSON and is never missing), or nothing (a cleared slot). So "{feature} is on but the set has no …" counts a linked or typed-path height, coat normal, fuzz, glow or subsurface and does not count a cleared one; "_cnr and _f both use slot 7" and "_g and _sk both use slot 3" need two own or borrowed files the JSON keeps, so a `slotN` on that slot (a link, a typed path or a clear) clears the clash, as the editor shows; and the "has no output path" check skips a slot whose `slotN` the JSON writes, a clear included (`slots_of` writes the string). On a set that writes no entry these read its own and borrowed fills, as before. The typed-path state changes a block for a set whose `slotN` names no set's output: Parallax on with a typed slot-4 path no set writes no longer blocks, and a typed slot-7 path over an own `_cnr` and `_f` no longer clashes.
  - **Dependencies and scope.** A link that names a set's output is a dependency exactly as a borrow is. Export set, Export group and Export N sets bring the link's set in as they bring a borrow's host (`with_hosts`, under the same excluded-group rule); the finalizer's dependencies of a JSON entry are the local files `json_files` names (`pbr_export::prepare`), so a linked file is checked like a borrowed one (written or up to date in this run, or on disk for a set left out on purpose, HF9) and the entry is held with the same note when it is not. An own or borrowed file that a `slotN` (a link, a typed path or a clear) overrides is not in the JSON, so it is no dependency of the entry; the set's own conversion jobs and a borrow's scope are unchanged. Tests `partial_export::a_scoped_export_treats_a_linked_lender_as_a_borrowed_one`, `an_excluded_linked_lender_holds_the_entry_as_a_borrowed_one_does` and `a_borrow_under_a_link_is_no_dependency`.
  - **The no-albedo Info.** Decided from the JSON the writer produces: an explicit `slot1` (a link, a typed path or a clear) is what the JSON names for slot 1, so the mesh keeps nothing and the "No albedo in this set; the mesh keeps {path}…" row (HL6) is not raised; `pbr_export::no_albedo_slot1` is `None` then, and `slots_of` writes the same JSON as before (the explicit `slot1` won already). Test `slot_links::with_a_linked_slot_1_the_no_albedo_info_is_not_raised`.

Review E's sets plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-16-review-e-sets.md`; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-16-egui037-review-e-design.md`, items E1, E13, E17, E24 and C14, and owner additions A1 and A2 of 2026-11-17). ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR10 No recipe combo (owner addition A1, 2026-11-17: "pbr is always rmaos, advanced skin is always rfaos, can be removed").** Rulings A1-1, A1-4, A1-5, A1-6, A1-7, A1-8, A1-9 and A2-5; a fuzz map follows the set's shader (owner: "fuzz is grayscale only for advanced skin, it should be treated according to which shader it is in", Note JQ).
  - The set editor has no RMAOS/RFAOS combo; the shader and the maps pick the recipe, and the packed row's slot title (RMAOS or RFAOS) shows the result. `packing.recipe` stays in the config, the engine, the CLI and the schemas. The selector's rules went with it: R-review I2's RFAOS option, its disabled reason and its re-click rule, and R-fills-2's "the recipe choice" (1.x); the digest's fills and skin-follow lines (cites R-fills-1, R-fills-2) stay.
  - **The model's guard stays.** `AppModel::set_packing_recipe` refuses RFAOS on a set that cannot build it (no roughness, glossiness or AO source, `rfaos_unbuildable`): it writes nothing, moves no fill and returns the reason, so the fills stay on the planned `_rmaos` key. Its one product caller is the sub-type edit's follow-on (`edit_subtype`, Note DQ: a diffuse made Advanced Skin takes its set's packed output to `_rfaos`, one taken off it goes back, in one step). There the sub-type change still lands, the refusal shows no message, and the packed row's title keeps RMAOS. The title carries the `KEEPS_RMAOS` hover and AccessKit description only on cs when the set's resolved shader is Advanced Skin; on a set whose shader stays PBR (an explicit PBR shader wins), it has no such hover. Test `packing_fills::rfaos_on_a_metallic_only_set_is_refused_with_the_reason`.
  - **PACK-UNUSED-INPUT** no longer says to change the recipe: "A packing input isn't used by this set — remove it, or change the set's shader or the file's type" (controller ruling: the combo the old sentence named is gone; `model/code_text.rs`).
  - **Clear recipe.** PACK-RECIPE-CONFLICT and PACK-RECIPE-UNBUILDABLE offer **Clear recipe** only when a recipe other than `auto` is written on either of the set's packed `[files]` keys (`_rmaos`, `_rfaos`) and the target is cs (`AppModel::recipe_written`); otherwise the row has no fix and keeps its sentence, as a recipe from the project, a preset or `--set` is not the button's to clear (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). The button and the effective file override use the same parser for a stored recipe. Its hover reads "Remove packing.recipe from this set's packed entries in sdc.toml. A project or preset recipe can apply next." The button removes `packing.recipe` from both packed entries in one undo step and prunes a `packing` table and a `[files."…"]` entry it leaves empty; fills stay, with their own CFG-FILE-UNMATCHED row where they have no file (A1-8).
  - **SHADER-EXTRA-MAP.** The row that names the set's packed output offers **Clear recipe**, then **Change shader…**, under the same condition (A1-7); otherwise **Change shader…** alone. It never offers **Remove from set**: a packed output cannot leave its set, and a button that can never work is noise (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). Navigation targets the packed member named by the warning, including complex and parallax packs with no RMAOS slot. The recipe fix still requires a matching RMAOS/RFAOS key. A non-packed extra member keeps **Remove from set**, then **Change shader…** (JO15).
  - On a read-only `sdc.toml` **Clear recipe** is disabled and its hover gives the read-only reason (A1-8); the command itself also returns early for an unrecognised packed key, a non-CS target, or a missing or read-only project document (A1-9). Undo restores the recipes removed from both keys. A metallic-only Advanced Skin set that keeps `_rmaos` (DQ2) says why on hover of its packed row's title (`set_editor::KEEPS_RMAOS`).
  - The standing exceptions stay: DQ2, a pinned diffuse (DW1), a pre-packed image (CO109), a plain `diffuse` picked by hand on an Advanced Skin set (DV3), and a written recipe still wins over an explicit shader (A1-7, Fuzz-20: a written `rfaos` under PBR keeps `_rfaos`, a written `rmaos` under Advanced Skin keeps `_rmaos`).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR14 Export reads what the JSON carries (final review C1, C2, I1, I2, I4; controller rulings 3b-F2 and 3b-F3, 2026-11-18).** Spec E1: a slot filled by a path from another set "is a filled slot everywhere", Export included. A set with no stored slot command (no `slotN` string: no link, no typed path, no clear) exports what main a0e2610c exports, JSON and DDS, byte for byte, wherever main exports (controller rulings 3b-F12 and 3b-F13; `partial_export::with_no_link_and_no_clear_export_blocks_and_writes_what_it_did`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`, `txst_map::a_stored_slot_key_keeps_the_record_normal_out_of_the_engine_inputs`). Two exceptions, both because a packed output's set is found by membership (since 2cf3a2e5): a set whose borrow displaced its own packed output, mapped to that output's default path, was a collision of the output with itself on main, which refused the project, and exports here (controller ruling 3b-F18, amending 3b-F3; `slot_links::a_pack_displaced_by_a_borrow_and_mapped_to_its_default_path_is_no_collision`); and on CS, when an excluded set `c` borrows slot 6 from a set `w` that has a legacy `output_name` and itself borrows slot 6 from `q`, `c`'s entry gets a `slot6` naming `w`'s own pack here (`textures\t\w_rmaos.dds` in the reviewed fixture, whose `output_name` is `w`, its default name; another legacy name changes the file name) and no `slot6` on main. Keep the second exception (3b-F23). In `slot_links::an_excluded_borrower_names_the_pack_written_under_its_lenders_legacy_name` the consumer’s albedo already exists. The named pack is absent before the scoped export and that export writes it.
  - **Path identity and the index.** A link is looked up with PGPatcher's own explicit-slot rule (`pgpsim` `slots.rs`, the `slot1`..`slot8` pass): ASCII lower case, `textures\` put in front unless the string starts with it (`txst_map::slot_path_key`), so `pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` and `textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` are one file and `other\textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` is not that file. Note HG's record paths keep `game_key`. The index (`txst_map::written_outputs`) holds every planned output of every set (each member that writes a file of its own: the members after the first of a slot kind and the outputs that fill no slot included), built in one pass with each output's kind (`slot_of`) and its set's short name; so on a settled plan every pick the From another set menu offers names an index key, and on a consumer that writes an entry (JR1) it is a link to the set that writes that path (the first in display order when two sets write it) and logs its Info line only on a changed pick (JR1). An output that fills no slot (a complex `_m`) is a local file with no kind, so it counts for no feature (JR8 needs a kind). An index key is the game location of the file the settled job writes on disk (`sets::written_game_location`): the job's output under the output folder or the export folder, its filesystem components (`.` and empty parts gone, as the writer resolves them) from the first `textures` component on (ASCII case-insensitive), joined with backslashes and ASCII-lowercased. The explicit path a mapping or prefix asks for is not read: the engine may settle on another file name (a mapped `rock_fuzz.png` typed `pbr_fuzz` writes `rock_f.dds`). It never starts from the manifest's job key (Unicode-lowercased, which loses a folder such as `İ`) and never splits a path as text. Path… strips leading separators, validates the relative DDS path and stores slashes as backslashes (3b-F32). A hand-written `slotN` goes straight to the explicit-slot lookup. From another set stores this path, spelt as on disk, and only on a settled plan (controller ruling 3b-F21, which withdraws 3b-F16's displayed prediction): `AppModel::pick_waits` waits on what Export waits on before it checks anything, a full plan or a scan-database read out (`full_plan_pending`, behind which `export_go` queues the export) and a plan that is not the one for these sets (`inputs_unsettled`, on which `plan_unsettled` refuses the export). After terminal delivery `plan_failed` holds picks and both export buttons until another plan starts. Export refuses before source or scope checks with “The last plan failed. Reopen the folder to plan it again before exporting.” This covers inputs equal to the failed request. The flag can also stay set after a later non-planning edit changes those inputs (3b-F27, 3b-F32). While it waits, every member row is off with one reason and no member's path is read, and a click (which checks again, `member_game_path`) stores nothing. The reason says which wait it is (controller ruling 3b-F25): "Planning: pick a file once the plan has finished" while a plan or a scan-database read runs, and "The plan must run again first: pick a file once a new plan has finished" when none runs but the plan is out of date or failed (a Path edit that changes a borrowed normal starts no plan. Picks and Export wait until another edit replans, 3b-F32). The rows read `pick_waits_cached` (controller ruling 3b-F24): a running plan or read is checked live, and the rest is computed once per `PickKey` (the views key, the plan generation, `sdc.toml`'s revision, `evidence_rev`, `mesh_rev` and `plan_failed`), so a menu kept open on frames where none of that changes builds no export inputs; the click's check in `member_game_path` is never cached. Export also plans again first when a source changed on disk (`check_sources`); a pick does not look at the files. The path comes from the settled job's output alone (`member_game_path_in`), never from an explicit path a pending edit asks for, so a pick never stores a path the producer does not write and an output with no game location keeps its error. The index never holds a prediction either, and `view_reads` holds each job's output, so a link resolved before a plan lands is resolved again when the output moves (controller ruling 3b-F16; `slot_links::a_link_is_recomputed_when_the_plan_moves_its_output_back`). A path is never composed from a source folder and an output name, and `slot_path_key` is applied on the lookup side only. The index finds the set that writes an output by membership (it walks each set's members), and the path function reads only the two folders and the item's settled output (`member_game_path_in(roots, item)`), so a packed output whose set borrows its slot, or one mapped to another folder, keeps its key. An output under no `textures` folder (a legacy `output_name`) has no game location: it is in no entry, and From another set disables it (`NO_GAME_PATH`). The member list and the index read the folders through `sets::output_roots` (one borrowed read of the cached export configuration and one of the export folder, over `AppModel::output_root_in`), once per menu call or index build, never per member; the export inputs read them once per pass, not per item (`export_rel_in`, `export_key_in`). A warm Assign call, including the row-cache key, copies zero `[export].skip` IDs (3b-F29). Reselecting the same folder changes the generation and terminal failure changes `plan_failed`, each invalidating the key even when the request inputs match (3b-F30). The read-count test pins a warm menu call with members at four reads of each with an Also tick on CS (two views lookups, the rows' cache key and `output_roots`), the same with more members and items; while a plan is out the list adds no read beyond the views lookups and builds no export inputs (controller rulings 3b-F6, 3b-F11, 3b-F14, 3b-F21 and 3b-F24, which withdraws 3b-F21's once-per-call bound; `slot_links::the_output_index_holds_where_each_file_is_written`, ten layouts through a real export, checked again with the consumer exported; `slot_links::the_slot_menu_builds_the_export_inputs_once_per_call`, `…::the_menu_and_the_index_read_the_folders_once_whatever_the_members`, `…::a_member_pick_waits_for_the_plan_and_then_stores_the_written_path`, `…::a_removed_mapping_offers_no_old_path_before_or_after_the_plan`, `…::a_member_pick_after_a_failed_replan_is_refused`, `…::an_open_slot_menu_builds_the_settled_check_once`, `…::the_settled_check_is_built_again_after_each_change_of_its_key`, `…::the_click_checks_the_plan_again_without_the_rows_cache`, `txst_map::with_the_plan_out_of_date_and_none_running_a_member_pick_says_it_must_plan_again`, `slot_menu::the_game_path_follows_the_current_prefix_not_the_last_plan`).
  - **One answer to "does this set write an entry".** `pbr_export::writes_entry` is `exports_json` (CS, a JSON set, a match that applies) and the statics rule keeping the entry (`pgp_entry_wanted`; a CS set with statics set to Never writes none). The link display, the feature defaults (`features`), the missing-map suppression and the cleared state read it through `link_slots`, and `written_json_entries` filters by it. On a set that writes no entry a stored link or clear changes no feature default and drops no missing-map row, and an own file under `slotN = ""` shows as itself (no dim chip, no "Left out of the JSON" sentence): `slot_shown`'s `Cleared` needs the file left out of `features`. `link_slots` asks `writes_entry` only of a set with a `slotN` string. The one exception is a record fill (controller ruling 3b-F13, withdrawing 3b-F9's record-fill clause): a stored `slotN` of any value keeps a TXST record from filling that slot (Note HG) on every set, a set that writes no entry included, as on main, because that decision feeds the engine's `borrowed_normal` input and so the roughness bake, and encoded bytes do not change. So on a statics-Never mapped set a typed path or a hand-edited `slotN` over a record-provided fill hides that fill in the editor, as on main (`slot_links::a_stored_slot_command_keeps_a_record_fill_out_as_on_main`, `txst_map::a_stored_slot_key_keeps_the_record_normal_out_of_the_engine_inputs`). Readers that promise the written result read `writes_entry` too: the no-albedo Info row; the 3D preview's candidate entry leaves the command out (JR7).
  - **Validation.** Export's blocks read what the JSON names (`sets::json_files`, over the fields the feature keys read: `feature_slots` and `linked`), in three states: a local file (an own or borrowed fill no `slotN` overrides, or a link that names a set's output, on the row it fills), a typed path no set writes (it fills its game slot for the JSON and is never missing), or nothing (a cleared slot). So "{feature} is on but the set has no …" counts a linked or typed-path height, coat normal, fuzz, glow or subsurface and does not count a cleared one; "_cnr and _f both use slot 7" and "_g and _sk both use slot 3" need two own or borrowed files the JSON keeps, so a `slotN` on that slot (a link, a typed path or a clear) clears the clash; and the "has no output path" check skips a slot whose `slotN` the JSON writes, a clear included (`slots_of` writes the string). One helper answers "does the JSON keep this fill" for the clash, the output-path check and the editor's conflict line with its Remove buttons (`sets::json_keeps_fill`, read by `view_row` without allocating), so on every set the editor shows a conflict only where the JSON keeps both fills: on a set that writes an entry, in the run or not, that is its effective slots (a `slotN` on the slot leaves no conflict); on a set that writes none (its statics drop it), its own and borrowed fills. Export checks a set in the run in its blocks, and a set left out of the run only in `prepare` and only when it has a stored slot command (its entry is held with the reason); a set left out with none is not checked (controller rulings 3b-F8, 3b-F12 and 3b-F17; `set_editor_ui::a_slot_the_json_overrides_shows_no_conflict_as_export_has_none`, `slot_links::export_takes_a_typed_path_over_a_clashing_slot_7_or_3`). On a set that writes no entry these read its own and borrowed fills, as before. The typed-path state changes a block for a set whose `slotN` names no set's output: Parallax on with a typed slot-4 path no set writes no longer blocks, and a typed slot-7 path over an own `_cnr` and `_f` no longer clashes.
  - **Dependencies and scope.** A link that names a set's output is a dependency exactly as a borrow is; scope and dependencies come from the link as `link_slots` resolved it, so a `slotN` string that names no settled output (a typed path, or a path the producer no longer writes) is an external path with no host and no dependency (controller ruling 3b-F5). Export set, Export group and Export N sets bring the link's set in as they bring a borrow's host (`with_hosts`, under the same excluded-group rule); they save the selected keys and expand hosts from the settled plan after any pending plan or source-triggered replan, before checking blocks or preparing JSON (3b-F20). A newly mirrored producer is therefore included. The finalizer's dependencies of a JSON entry are the local files `json_files` names (`pbr_export::prepare`). The run row is matched by its manifest job key. An untouched dependency is probed at the job’s exact output spelling, including Unicode. A failed row holds the entry even if an older file exists. A Vanilla borrow copy checks its captured source path (3b-F19). A linked file is checked like a borrowed one (written or up to date in this run, or on disk for a set left out on purpose, HF9) and the entry is held with the same note when it is not. An own or borrowed file that a `slotN` (a link, a typed path or a clear) overrides is not in the JSON, so it is no dependency of the entry; the set's own conversion jobs and a borrow's scope are unchanged. So a clear over a borrowed file never exported lets the entry in (`partial_export::a_clear_over_a_never_exported_borrow_lets_the_entry_in`). An entry of a set left out of the run gates nothing (HF9). A set with a stored slot command is written only when its own checks pass (the clash, missing-texture, feature-clash, prefix, slot-path and output-path blocks, `pbr_export::set_blocks`); otherwise it is held with "not in JSON: {set} ({reason})" and the run goes on. A set with none is not checked, as on main: its files on disk are accepted and its entry is written (controller rulings 3b-F7 and 3b-F12; `partial_export::an_entry_left_out_of_the_run_is_held_when_its_own_checks_refuse_it`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`). Tests `partial_export::a_scoped_export_treats_a_linked_lender_as_a_borrowed_one`, `an_excluded_linked_lender_holds_the_entry_as_a_borrowed_one_does` and `a_borrow_under_a_link_is_no_dependency`.
  - **The no-albedo Info.** Decided from the JSON the writer produces: an explicit `slot1` (a link, a typed path or a clear) is what the JSON names for slot 1, so the mesh keeps nothing and the "No albedo in this set; the mesh keeps {path}…" row (HL6) is not raised; `pbr_export::no_albedo_slot1` is `None` then, and `slots_of` writes the same JSON as before (the explicit `slot1` won already). Test `slot_links::with_a_linked_slot_1_the_no_albedo_info_is_not_raised`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR15 Accessible slot and fix names (final wave, Group 5).** A member chip exposes the file, pin, PGPatcher lock and link target in its description. A cleared button describes the file without an “empty” value. A typed-path button gives its linked path. A linked file label says “PGPatcher reads {path} for this slot.” Its “Open set {short} ({slot label})” button says “Open the set this file comes from.” The Match controls are “Match on this (Diffuse)” and “Match on this (Normal _n)”. A cut format exposes its full value. Output files names its button “Show {full path} in Explorer”. Problems names its fixes “Clear the packing recipe of {out} ({code})” and “Change shader for {id} ({code})” so two rows can be told apart.

Sets column wiki, Reading a set card (JR1, HE1), before the JR17 records correction:
A filled packed chip reads RMAOS or RFAOS according to the packed file.

The typed-path state changes a block for a set whose `slotN` names no set's output: Parallax on with a typed slot-4 path no set writes no longer blocks, and a typed slot-7 path over an own `_cnr` and `_f` no longer clashes.
  - **Dependencies and scope.** A link that names a set's output is a dependency exactly as a borrow is. Export set, Export group and Export N sets bring the link's set in as they bring a borrow's host (`with_hosts`, under the same excluded-group rule); the finalizer's dependencies of a JSON entry are the local files `json_files` names (`pbr_export::prepare`), so a linked file is checked like a borrowed one (written or up to date in this run, or on disk for a set left out on purpose, HF9) and the entry is held with the same note when it is not. An own or borrowed file that a `slotN` (a link, a typed path or a clear) overrides is not in the JSON, so it is no dependency of the entry; the set's own conversion jobs and a borrow's scope are unchanged. So a clear over a borrowed file never exported lets the entry in (`partial_export::a_clear_over_a_never_exported_borrow_lets_the_entry_in`). An entry of a set left out of the run gates nothing (HF9). A set with a stored slot command is written only when its own checks pass (the clash, missing-texture, feature-clash, prefix, slot-path and output-path blocks, `pbr_export::set_blocks`); otherwise it is held with "not in JSON: {set} ({reason})" and the run goes on. A set with none is not checked, as on main: its files on disk are accepted and its entry is written (controller rulings 3b-F7 and 3b-F12; `partial_export::an_entry_left_out_of_the_run_is_held_when_its_own_checks_refuse_it`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`). Tests `partial_export::a_scoped_export_treats_a_linked_lender_as_a_borrowed_one`, `an_excluded_linked_lender_holds_the_entry_as_a_borrowed_one_does` and `a_borrow_under_a_link_is_no_dependency`.
  - **The no-albedo Info.** Decided from the JSON the writer produces: an explicit `slot1` (a link, a typed path or a clear) is what the JSON names for slot 1, so the mesh keeps nothing and the "No albedo in this set; the mesh keeps {path}…" row (HL6) is not raised; `pbr_export::no_albedo_slot1` is `None` then, and `slots_of` writes the same JSON as before (the explicit `slot1` won already). Test `slot_links::with_a_linked_slot_1_the_no_albedo_info_is_not_raised`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR14 Export reads what the JSON carries (final review C1, C2, I1, I2, I4; controller rulings 3b-F2 and 3b-F3, 2026-11-18).** Spec E1: a slot filled by a path from another set "is a filled slot everywhere", Export included. A set with no stored slot command (no `slotN` string: no link, no typed path, no clear) exports what main a0e2610c exports, JSON and DDS, byte for byte, wherever main exports. A project main refused wrongly is outside that promise: a set whose borrow displaced its own packed output, mapped to that output's default path, was a collision of the output with itself on main and exports here, because its pack is found by membership (controller ruling 3b-F18, amending 3b-F3; `slot_links::a_pack_displaced_by_a_borrow_and_mapped_to_its_default_path_is_no_collision`) (controller rulings 3b-F12 and 3b-F13; `partial_export::with_no_link_and_no_clear_export_blocks_and_writes_what_it_did`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`, `txst_map::a_stored_slot_key_keeps_the_record_normal_out_of_the_engine_inputs`).
  - **Path identity and the index.** A link is looked up with PGPatcher's own explicit-slot rule (`pgpsim` `slots.rs`, the `slot1`..`slot8` pass): ASCII lower case, `textures\` put in front unless the string starts with it (`txst_map::slot_path_key`), so `pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` and `textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` are one file and `other\textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` is not that file. Note HG's record paths keep `game_key`. The index (`txst_map::written_outputs`) holds every planned output of every set (each member that writes a file of its own: the members after the first of a slot kind and the outputs that fill no slot included), built in one pass with each output's kind (`slot_of`) and its set's short name; so every pick the From another set menu offers is a link to its set and logs its Info line. An output that fills no slot (a complex `_m`) is a local file with no kind, so it counts for no feature (JR8 needs a kind). An index key is the game location of the file the settled job writes on disk (`sets::written_game_location`): the job's output under the output folder or the export folder, its filesystem components (`.` and empty parts gone, as the writer resolves them) from the first `textures` component on (ASCII case-insensitive), joined with backslashes and ASCII-lowercased. The explicit path a mapping or prefix asks for is not read: the engine may settle on another file name (a mapped `rock_fuzz.png` typed `pbr_fuzz` writes `rock_f.dds`). It never starts from the manifest's job key (Unicode-lowercased, which loses a folder such as `İ`) and never splits a path as text. From another set writes this path, spelt as on disk; Path… stores the text the user enters. Only while a plan is out (`full_plan_pending`) does From another set show the path the pending edit asks for, a prediction for display; the index never holds one, and `view_reads` holds each job's output, so a link resolved before a plan lands is resolved again when the output moves (controller ruling 3b-F16; `slot_links::a_link_is_recomputed_when_the_plan_moves_its_output_back`). A path is never composed from a source folder and an output name, and `slot_path_key` is applied on the lookup side only. The set that writes an output is found by membership and passed to the path function, so a packed output whose set borrows its slot, or one mapped to another folder, keeps its key. An output under no `textures` folder (a legacy `output_name`) has no game location: it is in no entry, and From another set disables it (`NO_GAME_PATH`). The menu reads the two folders once per call (`output_roots`) and builds the export inputs once per call and only while a plan is out (`member_game_path_in`); the index reads the folders once per build (controller rulings 3b-F6, 3b-F11, 3b-F14 and 3b-F16; `slot_links::the_output_index_holds_where_each_file_is_written`, ten layouts through a real export, checked again with the consumer exported; `slot_links::the_slot_menu_builds_the_export_inputs_once_per_call`).
  - **One answer to "does this set write an entry".** `pbr_export::writes_entry` is `exports_json` (CS, a JSON set, a match that applies) and the statics rule keeping the entry (`pgp_entry_wanted`; a CS set with statics set to Never writes none). The link display, the feature defaults (`features`), the missing-map suppression and the cleared state read it through `link_slots`, and `written_json_entries` filters by it. On a set that writes no entry a stored link or clear changes no feature default and drops no missing-map row, and an own file under `slotN = ""` shows as itself (no dim chip, no "Left out of the JSON" sentence): `slot_shown`'s `Cleared` needs the file left out of `features`. `link_slots` asks `writes_entry` only of a set with a `slotN` string. The one exception is a record fill (controller ruling 3b-F13, withdrawing 3b-F9's record-fill clause): a stored `slotN` of any value keeps a TXST record from filling that slot (Note HG) on every set, a set that writes no entry included, as on main, because that decision feeds the engine's `borrowed_normal` input and so the roughness bake, and encoded bytes do not change. So on a statics-Never mapped set a typed path or a hand-edited `slotN` over a record-provided fill hides that fill in the editor, as on main (`slot_links::a_stored_slot_command_keeps_a_record_fill_out_as_on_main`, `txst_map::a_stored_slot_key_keeps_the_record_normal_out_of_the_engine_inputs`). Readers that promise the written result read `writes_entry` too: the no-albedo Info row; the 3D preview's candidate entry leaves the command out (JR7).
  - **Validation.** Export's blocks read what the JSON names (`sets::json_files`, over the fields the feature keys read: `feature_slots` and `linked`), in three states: a local file (an own or borrowed fill no `slotN` overrides, or a link that names a set's output, on the row it fills), a typed path no set writes (it fills its game slot for the JSON and is never missing), or nothing (a cleared slot). So "{feature} is on but the set has no …" counts a linked or typed-path height, coat normal, fuzz, glow or subsurface and does not count a cleared one; "_cnr and _f both use slot 7" and "_g and _sk both use slot 3" need two own or borrowed files the JSON keeps, so a `slotN` on that slot (a link, a typed path or a clear) clears the clash; and the "has no output path" check skips a slot whose `slotN` the JSON writes, a clear included (`slots_of` writes the string). One helper answers "does the JSON keep this fill" for the clash, the output-path check and the editor's conflict line with its Remove buttons (`sets::json_keeps_fill`, read by `view_row` without allocating), so on every set the editor shows a conflict only where the JSON keeps both fills: on a set that writes an entry, in the run or not, that is its effective slots (a `slotN` on the slot leaves no conflict); on a set that writes none (its statics drop it), its own and borrowed fills. Export checks a set in the run in its blocks, and a set left out of the run only in `prepare` and only when it has a stored slot command (its entry is held with the reason); a set left out with none is not checked (controller rulings 3b-F8, 3b-F12 and 3b-F17; `set_editor_ui::a_slot_the_json_overrides_shows_no_conflict_as_export_has_none`, `slot_links::export_takes_a_typed_path_over_a_clashing_slot_7_or_3`). On a set that writes no entry these read its own and borrowed fills, as before. The typed-path state changes a block for a set whose `slotN` names no set's output: Parallax on with a typed slot-4 path no set writes no longer blocks, and a typed slot-7 path over an own `_cnr` and `_f` no longer clashes.
  - **Dependencies and scope.** A link that names a set's output is a dependency exactly as a borrow is. Export set, Export group and Export N sets bring the link's set in as they bring a borrow's host (`with_hosts`, under the same excluded-group rule); the finalizer's dependencies of a JSON entry are the local files `json_files` names (`pbr_export::prepare`), so a linked file is checked like a borrowed one (written or up to date in this run, or on disk for a set left out on purpose, HF9) and the entry is held with the same note when it is not. An own or borrowed file that a `slotN` (a link, a typed path or a clear) overrides is not in the JSON, so it is no dependency of the entry; the set's own conversion jobs and a borrow's scope are unchanged. So a clear over a borrowed file never exported lets the entry in (`partial_export::a_clear_over_a_never_exported_borrow_lets_the_entry_in`). An entry of a set left out of the run gates nothing (HF9). A set with a stored slot command is written only when its own checks pass (the clash, missing-texture, feature-clash, prefix, slot-path and output-path blocks, `pbr_export::set_blocks`); otherwise it is held with "not in JSON: {set} ({reason})" and the run goes on. A set with none is not checked, as on main: its files on disk are accepted and its entry is written (controller rulings 3b-F7 and 3b-F12; `partial_export::an_entry_left_out_of_the_run_is_held_when_its_own_checks_refuse_it`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`). Tests `partial_export::a_scoped_export_treats_a_linked_lender_as_a_borrowed_one`, `an_excluded_linked_lender_holds_the_entry_as_a_borrowed_one_does` and `a_borrow_under_a_link_is_no_dependency`.
  - **The no-albedo Info.** Decided from the JSON the writer produces: an explicit `slot1` (a link, a typed path or a clear) is what the JSON names for slot 1, so the mesh keeps nothing and the "No albedo in this set; the mesh keeps {path}…" row (HL6) is not raised; `pbr_export::no_albedo_slot1` is `None` then, and `slots_of` writes the same JSON as before (the explicit `slot1` won already). Test `slot_links::with_a_linked_slot_1_the_no_albedo_info_is_not_raised`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR1 A `slotN` with a non-empty value is a link (E1).** It is shown only on a set that writes a PGPatcher entry (`pbr_export::writes_entry`: a JSON set whose statics keep the entry, JR14). A link sits on the row of its game slot (`link.row`): the source item's kind when that kind's game slot is the link's slot; else the kind the set already has on that slot; else the slot's default kind (1 Diffuse, 2 Normal, 3 Emissive, 4 Height, 6 RMAOS, 7 Coat, 8 Subsurface; `sets::link_row_kind`). On game slot 5 with no kind of the set there it has no row. So a slot-7 link is on the Coat or the Fuzz row, never both, and a path to another kind reads on the slot's own row (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). One precedence rule, `sets::slot_shown` (the one display reader; `v.slots` keeps its meaning): a link on the row, then an own or borrowed file whose game slot holds a link on another row or on none (`Replaced`), then an own or borrowed file under `slotN = ""` on a set that writes an entry (`Cleared`), then own or borrowed, then the record, then empty. The row shows what the JSON carries.
  - **The row.** A link reads as filled: "{file}" and the link "from set {short}" that opens that set (AccessKit "Open set {short} ({slot label})", hover "Open the set this file comes from."), or "{file} · set by path"; the button **Unlink**; no format, alpha or channel-source controls and no status dot. Both sides stay inside the File cell: the file name is cut at the end, the link side takes at most half of the cell and is cut at its end, each with its full text on hover, and a linked row never makes the File column wider (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). On a shared game slot (3 or 7) a linked slot's row belongs to the link's kind, whether or not the set has an own file there, and shows **Unlink**, never Assign… (`sets::view_row`, which `editor_cells` and the status line's slot reasons build on). "Match on this" is not drawn on a linked slot 1 or 2; the Matching group keeps it. **Unlink** and **Use this file** are off on a pinned slot, with the slot menu's reason (`AppModel::slot_command_off`). A link to a slot that has no row (no shader pick and no own file there) draws no row (`// shortcut:` in `view_row`).
  - **Under a link.** An own file under a link for another kind (slot 7: an `_f` under a link to a `_cnr`) does not read as filled: the row shows the link and "{file} is not written: slot {n} is linked.", no chip is drawn for it and the chip count does not count it, and the 3D preview does not serve it (controller ruling 2, round 2: "when a link holds a game slot, an own or borrowed file of a different kind in that same game slot is not in the JSON, so it does not read as filled"; controller rulings, round 3: (1) "a Replaced slot draws no chip, the linked kind's chip carries the link"; (2) "when a game slot is linked its shared row belongs to the link's kind, and it shows Unlink"). On a set that writes an entry, a `slotN = ""` over an own or borrowed file reads "Left out of the JSON: you cleared this slot." with **Use this file**; on any other set no JSON carries the clear, and the file shows as itself (JR14). The Assign menu keeps **Clear**. A link picked from the Assign menu logs one Info line to Messages ("{slot label} of {set short}: {file} (from set {from short})").
  - **The chip.** A linked chip is filled and named "{letter}, from set {short}" ("{letter}, path {file}" for a typed path), hover "{slot} linked to {path}", with the link mark painted in a 10 px square at the chip's top centre, between the two lock cells, in every lock state (controller ruling, 2026-11-18); the locks stay on a linked chip. A cleared slot's chip is the dim letter box "{letter}, cleared", hover "{slot} (cleared): {file}". An own file whose slot is linked (of any kind) or cleared draws no trailing thumbnail, and `chip_rows` counts what is drawn (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). The cleared chip and the typed-path box sense a click for the slot menu, so they are `Role::Button` and Tab stops (egui-ui-rules 8.5).
  - **The missing-map check.** A `SHADER-MISSING-MAP` warning for a slot that holds a link with a row is dropped (`link_slots`): the JSON names a file for it.
  - **Resolving.** `txst_map::link_slots` finds a link's source in the index of every planned output, by the game location of the file each job writes (`sets::output_game_path`) under PGPatcher's slot path rule (JR14), so the opened folder does not matter; the first set in display order that writes a path keeps it. The JSON's slot paths are unchanged (`slotN` already won); the feature keys follow JR8.

It also waits after a plan failed for good (`plan_failed`, set where that error is raised and cleared when the next plan starts), where Export's two waits do not fire: no plan is out, and the inputs equal the failed plan's request. The menu and the index read the export configuration and the export folder once per call or build (`sets::output_roots`, over `AppModel::output_root_in`), never per member. The menu's settled check builds the export inputs once per call (`EngineInputs::of`), and they read the folders once per pass, not per item (`export_rel_in`, `export_key_in`); the views lookups and that check still read them a fixed number of times per call, whatever the number of members and items.
