The typed-path state changes a block for a set whose `slotN` names no set's output: Parallax on with a typed slot-4 path no set writes no longer blocks, and a typed slot-7 path over an own `_cnr` and `_f` no longer clashes.
  - **Dependencies and scope.** A link that names a set's output is a dependency exactly as a borrow is; scope and dependencies come from the link as `link_slots` resolved it, so a `slotN` string that names no settled output (a typed path, or a path the producer no longer writes) is an external path with no host and no dependency (controller ruling 3b-F5). Export set, Export group and Export N sets bring the link's set in as they bring a borrow's host (`with_hosts`, under the same excluded-group rule); they read the links of the plan on screen before they wait for a pending plan, so a producer only the landing plan names stays out of the scope and the consumer's entry is held with "not exported yet" (controller ruling 3b-F20, parked for the final fix wave); the finalizer's dependencies of a JSON entry are the local files `json_files` names (`pbr_export::prepare`), so a linked file is checked like a borrowed one (written or up to date in this run, or on disk for a set left out on purpose, HF9) and the entry is held with the same note when it is not. An own or borrowed file that a `slotN` (a link, a typed path or a clear) overrides is not in the JSON, so it is no dependency of the entry; the set's own conversion jobs and a borrow's scope are unchanged. So a clear over a borrowed file never exported lets the entry in (`partial_export::a_clear_over_a_never_exported_borrow_lets_the_entry_in`). An entry of a set left out of the run gates nothing (HF9). A set with a stored slot command is written only when its own checks pass (the clash, missing-texture, feature-clash, prefix, slot-path and output-path blocks, `pbr_export::set_blocks`); otherwise it is held with "not in JSON: {set} ({reason})" and the run goes on. A set with none is not checked, as on main: its files on disk are accepted and its entry is written (controller rulings 3b-F7 and 3b-F12; `partial_export::an_entry_left_out_of_the_run_is_held_when_its_own_checks_refuse_it`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`). Tests `partial_export::a_scoped_export_treats_a_linked_lender_as_a_borrowed_one`, `an_excluded_linked_lender_holds_the_entry_as_a_borrowed_one_does` and `a_borrow_under_a_link_is_no_dependency`.
  - **The no-albedo Info.** Decided from the JSON the writer produces: an explicit `slot1` (a link, a typed path or a clear) is what the JSON names for slot 1, so the mesh keeps nothing and the "No albedo in this set; the mesh keeps {path}…" row (HL6) is not raised; `pbr_export::no_albedo_slot1` is `None` then, and `slots_of` writes the same JSON as before (the explicit `slot1` won already). Test `slot_links::with_a_linked_slot_1_the_no_albedo_info_is_not_raised`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR1 A `slotN` with a non-empty value is a link (E1).** It is shown only on a set that writes a PGPatcher entry (`pbr_export::writes_entry`: a JSON set whose statics keep the entry, JR14). A link sits on the row of its game slot (`link.row`): the source item's kind when that kind's game slot is the link's slot; else the kind the set already has on that slot; else the slot's default kind (1 Diffuse, 2 Normal, 3 Emissive, 4 Height, 6 RMAOS, 7 Coat, 8 Subsurface; `sets::link_row_kind`). On game slot 5 with no kind of the set there it has no row. So a slot-7 link is on the Coat or the Fuzz row, never both, and a path to another kind reads on the slot's own row (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). One precedence rule, `sets::slot_shown` (the one display reader; `v.slots` keeps its meaning): a link on the row, then an own or borrowed file whose game slot holds a link on another row or on none (`Replaced`), then an own or borrowed file under `slotN = ""` on a set that writes an entry (`Cleared`), then own or borrowed, then the record, then empty. The row shows what the JSON carries.
  - **The row.** A link reads as filled: "{file}" and the link "from set {short}" that opens that set (AccessKit "Open set {short} ({slot label})", hover "Open the set this file comes from."), or "{file} · set by path"; the button **Unlink**; no format, alpha or channel-source controls and no status dot. Both sides stay inside the File cell: the file name is cut at the end, the link side takes at most half of the cell and is cut at its end, each with its full text on hover, and a linked row never makes the File column wider (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). On a shared game slot (3 or 7) a linked slot's row belongs to the link's kind, whether or not the set has an own file there, and shows **Unlink**, never Assign… (`sets::view_row`, which `editor_cells` and the status line's slot reasons build on). "Match on this" is not drawn on a linked slot 1 or 2; the Matching group keeps it. **Unlink** and **Use this file** are off on a pinned slot, with the slot menu's reason (`AppModel::slot_command_off`). A link to a slot that has no row (no shader pick and no own file there) draws no row (`// shortcut:` in `view_row`).
  - **Under a link.** An own file under a link for another kind (slot 7: an `_f` under a link to a `_cnr`) does not read as filled: the row shows the link and "{file} is not written: slot {n} is linked.", no chip is drawn for it and the chip count does not count it, and the 3D preview does not serve it (controller ruling 2, round 2: "when a link holds a game slot, an own or borrowed file of a different kind in that same game slot is not in the JSON, so it does not read as filled"; controller rulings, round 3: (1) "a Replaced slot draws no chip, the linked kind's chip carries the link"; (2) "when a game slot is linked its shared row belongs to the link's kind, and it shows Unlink"). On a set that writes an entry, a `slotN = ""` over an own or borrowed file reads "Left out of the JSON: you cleared this slot." with **Use this file**; on any other set no JSON carries the clear, and the file shows as itself (JR14). The Assign menu keeps **Clear**. A link picked from the Assign menu logs one Info line to Messages ("{slot label} of {set short}: {file} (from set {from short})").
  - **The chip.** A linked chip is filled and named "{letter}, from set {short}" ("{letter}, path {file}" for a typed path), hover "{slot} linked to {path}", with the link mark painted in a 10 px square at the chip's top centre, between the two lock cells, in every lock state (controller ruling, 2026-11-18); the locks stay on a linked chip. A cleared slot's chip is the dim letter box "{letter}, cleared", hover "{slot} (cleared): {file}". An own file whose slot is linked (of any kind) or cleared draws no trailing thumbnail, and `chip_rows` counts what is drawn (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). The cleared chip and the typed-path box sense a click for the slot menu, so they are `Role::Button` and Tab stops (egui-ui-rules 8.5).
  - **The missing-map check.** A `SHADER-MISSING-MAP` warning for a slot that holds a link with a row is dropped (`link_slots`): the JSON names a file for it.
  - **Resolving.** `txst_map::link_slots` finds a link's source in the index of every planned output, by the game location of the file each settled job writes (`sets::written_game_location`, JR14) under PGPatcher's slot path rule (JR14), so the opened folder does not matter; the first set in display order that writes a path keeps it. The JSON's slot paths are unchanged (`slotN` already won); the feature keys follow JR8.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR2 `SetView.short`.** The shortest unique tail of a set id, of at least two parts, compared folded (an id of one part is itself, and ids equal after folding keep the whole id; `sets::short_names`). The card titles (JR5), the "from set" texts and the linked chip's name use it.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR3 The "Output files" group (E13).** Under the set editor's title, in 2D only (the 3D view keeps its height for the viewport, which `set_preview_layout::in_3d_the_viewport_takes_most_of_the_centre` pins), a group lists every file the set's own members write (`SetView.outputs`, `sets::set_outputs`): each own slot's planned output in slot order, then each member with a job that fills no slot (a complex `_m`, an unslotted packed output) in member order, labelled with its name, each path once. An own file under a linked slot stays listed, as it still converts; a lender's file is not (controller rulings, 2026-11-18: the list is what the export writes, and the 3D view is not given the group). Each line has **Show in Explorer** once the file exists. The group is closed by default and its header summarises ("{n}, all written" / "{n}, not exported yet" / "{k} of {n} written" / "none"); an empty list reads "Nothing is written for this set." The `→ prefix` arrow shows only for a PBR set (`SetView::pbr`), whose textures are written under the prefix; a prefix error shows on every JSON set.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR4 Values are never cut for their labels (E17; owner addition A2).** This departs from spec 7a.4's in-value labels on controller ruling 1, round 2: "A value is never cut to make room for its own label. … At 1280 every format, recipe, source and alpha value reads in full, and a test asserts it. … if the slot rows form columns, put the words in one heading line above them"; it is narrowed for source names by owner addition A2 and rulings A2-1…A2-4, A2-6 and A2-7, 2026-11-17: "if text cropping needs to occur, it should happen at the end, so you can see the suffix", read as "the visible part keeps the END of the file name".
  - **Heading.** The slot rows are a 6-column grid (Slot, thumbnail, File, Format, Flags, Status), so the labels are one heading line above them ("Slot", "File", "Format", "Alpha"; the thumbnail and status columns have none). It is drawn only when some row has a format combo, and "Alpha" only over a Diffuse row's alpha combo, with its left edge at the combo's: on a set whose Diffuse row draws the Match toggle first, the heading cell starts after the toggle's width, so Match has no heading (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). Every combo paints its bare value; the labels are in each combo's AccessKit name ("Normal _n format", "Slot alpha type: {label}").
  - **Widths.** The format combos keep `COMBO_W` (96) as their minimum and widen into the room left in their column and the row (`combo_w`; the slack inside the column is `(format_base - COMBO_W).max(0)`); a format value that is still cut has its full value on hover. The alpha combo stays `ALPHA_W`, which holds "Cutout + Blend". `ROWS_MIN` stays 724, so the labels cost the side columns no additional width (opening a set still narrows them to the floor, HP6). Budgets calculated from the constants, not measured breakpoints: 143 (Textures) + 133 (Sets) + 724 = 1000 fits 1024; with the default side columns (380 + 266 = 646 px) the editor is at that floor up to a 1370 px window. At 1280 every format and alpha value reads in full (`packing_fills::a_long_format_value_reads_in_full_at_1280_and_1536`, `slot_alpha::the_alpha_value_reads_in_full_at_1280`, `…_on_a_json_set`).
  - **Channel lines.** The channel lines of a packed output span the Format cell and the row's empty Flags cell; every line has a role-label column as wide as the widest role of that output, so all its pickers start at one x, and every picker is `SOURCE_W` 130 wide (probe P2b: `…_roughness.png` needs 126.1 px; ruling A2-4 keeps `COMBO_W` 96). This supersedes DY18's `SOURCE_W` (1.x). A source file name longer than the picker is cut at the front (`front_cut`, `…_roughness.png`), so its suffix shows, with the full name on hover and in the AccessKit value; a picker with no file paints "value" and its hover reads "No file: the box sets this channel (0 to 255).". On a read-only `sdc.toml` a disabled picker's hover is two lines, the full name (or the Value sentence) and then the read-only reason (ruling A2-6); the value box and **Reset** give the reason on hover too. The lines' Flags width is read from the one store `rows` already keeps, `"slot-rows-others"` (ruling A2-7). The painted "Source: " is gone; the AccessKit value keeps it (A2-2). File names are user text of any length, while formats and alpha modes are short fixed strings.
  - **Match.** The Match toggle is the painted link mark alone (`MARK_W` 13, the chip's mark), with its AccessKit name "Match on this" and its hover unchanged; the word "Match" is not drawn. Controller ruling, 2026-11-18: spec E17 asks for "a visible label or a named tooltip" and the toggle has the named tooltip; drawn from 900 px up, the word would take 41 px from File and move the Format column off `look_036`'s pin. The owner may ask for the word. No emoji glyph in any label. This supersedes in part FB2's (1.x) "icon-toggle flags "Match on this" and "Opaque"" (Opaque is JI5's dropdown).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR5 Floors and card titles (E24).** The centre's floors hold only while the Selection tab is in front: the 2D set editor's (`ROWS_MIN`, 724 px) and an expanded texture's (`ui::centre_floor`: `PREVIEW_MIN` 480 + the open settings' 280 + 16 = 776 px in a window 1280 px or wider, where the settings start open; 480 + the 24 px strip + 16 = 520 px below). Behind Presets, the JSON editor or Settings the floor is `CENTRE_MIN` (360), so the side columns keep their widths behind a centre tab, and coming back to Selection takes the floor again (controller ruling, 2026-11-18, for the preview's floor). This supersedes in part (1.x) FB2's "while the 2D set editor is shown the centre's floor is `set_editor::ROWS_MIN`", FC8's "from 1280 the centre's floor is `PREVIEW_MIN + SETTINGS_WIDTH + 16` (776)" and CE15's 520 px floor: each holds only while Selection is in front. The 3D settings window reads the front tab after the centre is drawn, so it never draws a frame over a tab (AG132). Card titles keep the set's unique tail (JR2): the middle forms are used only when they end with it, the last form is `…/<tail>` (the whole id when the tail is the whole id), a title that does not fit beside the status pill takes its own line, and it wraps only when that last form does not fit on its own line either (HN6, HP7; controller ruling, 2026-11-18).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR6 A feature's state shows once (C14).** The four feature groups with a Features tick (Coat, Emissive, Glint, Fuzz) carry no on/off word in their header: the tick, drawn on every JSON set, says it. A group whose state shows only in a control inside its own body (Subsurface's mode selector, the XPMF card's checkbox) says that state in its header while the group is closed and nothing while it is open; "closed" is the real collapse state (`group::group_closed_value`), not the default. A blocked feature keeps its amber reason, `{state}, blocked by {feature}`, open or closed (CC10). This narrows "a closed header is never silent" to headers whose state shows nowhere else (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). FM33's kept group follows it. This supersedes in part (1.x) CC8's header values (Subsurface "Off, Standard, Foliage"; Coat, Emissive, Parallax, Glint, Fuzz "on" or "off"), FB2's "A feature's state shows once, in its own group header ("on" / "off")" and AJ4's kept Coat group "reading "off"".

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR7 The 3D preview serves a link (E1, 3D).** `set_preview::member_slots` serves a linked slot's file at the link's path, keyed by the row the link fills (`link.row`), and does not serve that row's own fill. A link with an item but no row (game slot 5 with no kind of the set on it) is not served (`// shortcut:`), and a typed path that names no set output pushes nothing, so the own fill keeps its prefix path. An own file under a link for another kind (`Replaced`) is not served (JR1). Links are read only for a set with a JSON entry. The simulator is given the set's candidate entry (`pbr_export::json_entries`, read by `set_preview::fingerprint`), so a set whose statics drop its entry is still previewed; on such a set the candidate carries no stored `slotN` link or clear, as its written result would not (controller ruling 3b-F9; `slot_links::the_preview_of_a_set_that_writes_no_entry_simulates_no_stored_slot_command`). The member key is not `link.kind`, because `Encoder.members` is keyed by kind and a link to another slot's kind would evict the set's own fill of that kind (controller ruling, 2026-11-18).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR8 A linked slot counts for the features (owner, 2026-11-16, "4 yes").** Height and parallax by controller ruling 3, round 2: "A linked height turns `parallax` on … make the `checks.rs` `filled` reader … treat a linked height as filled"; slot 7 by controller ruling 2, round 2, quoted in JR1. A slot linked from another set (resolved to a source set, an item and a kind) counts for the feature defaults (`auto_flags`) and for the coat, fuzz and subsurface blocks as the kind of the row it fills (`link.row`, JR1), in place of the set's own and borrowed fills of that game slot: the game reads a slot by its number, so the feature is the slot's (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). A typed path that names no set output does not count. A counting link with no row (game slot 5) removes the slot's own fills and adds none (`// shortcut:`). The readers take `sets::feature_slots` (`SetView.features`, built by `link_slots`): the PGPatcher JSON writer, `checks.rs` and `pgp_fields::context_of`. A link changes the input of the feature defaults (`auto_flags_for`), not the keys themselves: it defaults a key on as an own file of that kind would, `emissive` (`_g`), `parallax` (`_p`, height), `multilayer` and `coat_normal` (`_cnr`), `fuzz_on` (`_f`, written as the `fuzz` object), and for `_s` `subsurface` or `subsurface_foliage`, or `coat_diffuse` while multilayer is on. A default never replaces a key the set writes itself (`false` included), a feature another feature blocks gets none, and on a PBR grass set only `_s` switches a key on (AS8); so the exported JSON gains the key only where a default would apply. A linked height counts as filled for the checks: a keyed Landscape or PBR Landscape set with one joins CHK-PARALLAX-SCALE's comparison of displacement scales, which reads no height pixels. A link whose source set is gone no longer counts. This supersedes in part AJ7 ("member textures switch features on") and AJ11 ("a filled coat slot", "a filled fuzz slot") (1.x; the digest's coat line says so).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR9 A cleared slot turns no feature on.** Controller ruling 5, round 3: "an own file under `slotN = ""` turns no feature on, which changes exported JSON for such sets: the controller's call, for the owner to overrule". An own or borrowed file under `slotN = ""` is left out of the JSON, so `feature_slots` drops every kind of that game slot and the file no longer defaults its feature on (`emissive` for a cleared slot 3, and so on); a key the set writes itself stays. This holds only for a set that writes a PGPatcher entry (`features` is computed where `writes_entry` holds, as `linked` is: the statics decision included, JR14); a set without one writes no `slotN`, so its clear changes nothing. A clear counts only where it removes an own or borrowed fill. For such JSON sets the other `feature_slots` readers follow: a cleared height takes a Landscape or PBR Landscape set out of CHK-PARALLAX-SCALE's comparison, and a cleared `_cnr` or `_f` blocks nothing. The exported JSON changes for a set whose cleared slot (3, 4, 7, 8) held a file that defaulted a feature on; no pinned test changed.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR10 No recipe combo (owner addition A1, 2026-11-17: "pbr is always rmaos, advanced skin is always rfaos, can be removed").** Rulings A1-1, A1-4, A1-5, A1-6, A1-7, A1-8, A1-9 and A2-5; a fuzz map follows the set's shader (owner: "fuzz is grayscale only for advanced skin, it should be treated according to which shader it is in", Note JQ).
  - The set editor has no RMAOS/RFAOS combo; the shader and the maps pick the recipe, and the packed row's slot title (RMAOS or RFAOS) shows the result. `packing.recipe` stays in the config, the engine, the CLI and the schemas. The selector's rules went with it: R-review I2's RFAOS option, its disabled reason and its re-click rule, and R-fills-2's "the recipe choice" (1.x); the digest's fills and skin-follow lines (cites R-fills-1, R-fills-2) stay.
  - **The model's guard stays.** `AppModel::set_packing_recipe` refuses RFAOS on a set that cannot build it (no roughness, glossiness or AO source, `rfaos_unbuildable`): it writes nothing, moves no fill and returns the reason, so the fills stay on the planned `_rmaos` key. Its one product caller is the sub-type edit's follow-on (`edit_subtype`, Note DQ: a diffuse made Advanced Skin takes its set's packed output to `_rfaos`, one taken off it goes back, in one step). There the sub-type change still lands, the refusal shows no message, and the packed row's title keeps RMAOS. The title carries the `KEEPS_RMAOS` hover and AccessKit description only on cs when the set's resolved shader is Advanced Skin; on a set whose shader stays PBR (an explicit PBR shader wins), it has no such hover. Test `packing_fills::rfaos_on_a_metallic_only_set_is_refused_with_the_reason`.
  - **PACK-UNUSED-INPUT** no longer says to change the recipe: "A packing input isn't used by this set — remove it, or change the set's shader or the file's type" (controller ruling: the combo the old sentence named is gone; `model/code_text.rs`).
  - **Clear recipe.** PACK-RECIPE-CONFLICT and PACK-RECIPE-UNBUILDABLE offer **Clear recipe** only when a recipe other than `auto` is written on either of the set's packed `[files]` keys (`_rmaos`, `_rfaos`) and the target is cs (`AppModel::recipe_written`); otherwise the row has no fix and keeps its sentence, as a recipe from the project, a preset or `--set` is not the button's to clear (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). The button removes `packing.recipe` from both packed entries in one undo step and prunes a `packing` table and a `[files."…"]` entry it leaves empty; fills stay, with their own CFG-FILE-UNMATCHED row where they have no file (A1-8).
  - **SHADER-EXTRA-MAP.** The row that names the set's packed output offers **Clear recipe**, then **Change shader…**, under the same condition (A1-7); otherwise **Change shader…** alone. It never offers **Remove from set**: a packed output cannot leave its set, and a button that can never work is noise (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). A non-packed extra member keeps **Remove from set**, then **Change shader…** (JO15).
  - On a read-only `sdc.toml` **Clear recipe** is disabled and its hover gives the read-only reason (A1-8); there the disabled button is the guard, and the model's own refusal is `ProjectDoc`'s (A1-9). A metallic-only Advanced Skin set that keeps `_rmaos` (DQ2) says why on hover of its packed row's title (`set_editor::KEEPS_RMAOS`).
  - The standing exceptions stay: DQ2, a pinned diffuse (DW1), a pre-packed image (CO109), a plain `diffuse` picked by hand on an Advanced Skin set (DV3), and a written recipe still wins over an explicit shader (A1-7, Fuzz-20: a written `rfaos` under PBR keeps `_rfaos`, a written `rmaos` under Advanced Skin keeps `_rmaos`).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR11 A set filter follows its set (follow-up F7; planner's call and controller rulings, 2026-11-18).** `bottom::view_of` (over `view_lookup`, which holds the lookup order once) finds, after the key, the key-less id and the folded id (JP5), the key-less set whose `engine::sets::set_key(sets root, id)` is the stored value, so a Problems filter opened by a set's key follows the set when the set loses every file and with it its key. When the output root changes, the filter's stored value (`Set`, `SetNotes`, `SetChecks`) is rekeyed to the new root as the selected set is (JI2; `AppModel::rekey_filter` in `model/export_scope.rs`) when it names a set by key, and left as it is when it names a set by its id. Which it names is decided by the lookup under the plan before the change (`view_lookup`, `AppModel::set_filter_names_an_id`: the second and third lookups name an id), since the text cannot tell a typed id from a key. The filter's chip resolves through the same lookup (`bottom::scope_label`). Tests `problems_notes::a_keyed_sets_filter_follows_the_set_once_it_loses_its_key`, `a_set_filter_keeps_its_set_when_the_root_moves_to_an_ancestor`, `a_set_filter_keeps_its_set_when_the_root_changes` and `a_key_less_ids_filter_is_not_rekeyed_when_the_root_changes`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR12 The new view fields hold after a partial plan as after a full build (planner's call).** `short` is carried over by `splice_views` (a splice never changes the list of set ids), `outputs` is set in `finish_view`, which both paths run, and `linked` / `features` are built by `link_slots`. `build_views` runs it over every view, and `splice_views` runs it over the views it rebuilds. `splice_views`' mapped-project shortcut is also taken when any sets-doc entry has a non-empty string `slotN`, so the splice path only meets `slotN = ""` clears; the shortcut's comment says to upgrade "when a splice can name the sets a link reads" (controller ruling, 2026-11-18). `view_reads` holds each item's `job_key`, so a moved source output rebuilds its links. Pinned by `fast_replan::views_after_a_partial_equal_a_fresh_build`, `…_with_a_linked_slot` and `…_with_a_cleared_slot`. 3a's `sets::editor_cells` is built on `view_row`, so the editor's rows and the status line's slot reasons follow a link's kind on slots 3 and 7 together (JO7).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JR, 2026-11-19)
- **JR14 Export reads what the JSON carries (final review C1, C2, I1, I2, I4; controller rulings 3b-F2 and 3b-F3, 2026-11-18).** Spec E1: a slot filled by a path from another set "is a filled slot everywhere", Export included. A set with no stored slot command (no `slotN` string: no link, no typed path, no clear) exports what main a0e2610c exports, JSON and DDS, byte for byte, wherever main exports (controller rulings 3b-F12 and 3b-F13; `partial_export::with_no_link_and_no_clear_export_blocks_and_writes_what_it_did`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`, `txst_map::a_stored_slot_key_keeps_the_record_normal_out_of_the_engine_inputs`). Two exceptions, both because a packed output's set is found by membership (since 2cf3a2e5): a set whose borrow displaced its own packed output, mapped to that output's default path, was a collision of the output with itself on main, which refused the project, and exports here (controller ruling 3b-F18, amending 3b-F3; `slot_links::a_pack_displaced_by_a_borrow_and_mapped_to_its_default_path_is_no_collision`); and on CS, when an excluded set `c` borrows slot 6 from a set `w` that has a legacy `output_name` and itself borrows slot 6 from `q`, `c`'s entry gets a `slot6` naming `w`'s own pack here (`textures\t\w_rmaos.dds` in the reviewed fixture, whose `output_name` is `w`, its default name; another legacy name changes the file name) and no `slot6` on main. The second is parked for the branch's final fix wave, which pins it with a test that the file that line names is written, or else makes the entry main's (controller ruling 3b-F23).
  - **Path identity and the index.** A link is looked up with PGPatcher's own explicit-slot rule (`pgpsim` `slots.rs`, the `slot1`..`slot8` pass): ASCII lower case, `textures\` put in front unless the string starts with it (`txst_map::slot_path_key`), so `pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` and `textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` are one file and `other\textures\pbr\arch\wall_n.dds` is not that file. Note HG's record paths keep `game_key`. The index (`txst_map::written_outputs`) holds every planned output of every set (each member that writes a file of its own: the members after the first of a slot kind and the outputs that fill no slot included), built in one pass with each output's kind (`slot_of`) and its set's short name; so on a settled plan every pick the From another set menu offers names an index key, and on a consumer that writes an entry (JR1) it is a link to the set that writes that path (the first in display order when two sets write it) and logs its Info line. An output that fills no slot (a complex `_m`) is a local file with no kind, so it counts for no feature (JR8 needs a kind). An index key is the game location of the file the settled job writes on disk (`sets::written_game_location`): the job's output under the output folder or the export folder, its filesystem components (`.` and empty parts gone, as the writer resolves them) from the first `textures` component on (ASCII case-insensitive), joined with backslashes and ASCII-lowercased. The explicit path a mapping or prefix asks for is not read: the engine may settle on another file name (a mapped `rock_fuzz.png` typed `pbr_fuzz` writes `rock_f.dds`). It never starts from the manifest's job key (Unicode-lowercased, which loses a folder such as `İ`) and never splits a path as text. Path… stores the text the user enters. From another set stores this path, spelt as on disk, and only on a settled plan (controller ruling 3b-F21, which withdraws 3b-F16's displayed prediction): `AppModel::pick_waits` waits on what Export waits on before it checks anything, a full plan or a scan-database read out (`full_plan_pending`, behind which `export_go` queues the export) and a plan that is not the one for these sets (`inputs_unsettled`, on which `plan_unsettled` refuses the export). It also waits after a plan failed for good (`plan_failed`, set where that error is raised and cleared when the next plan starts), where Export's two waits do not fire: no plan is out, and the inputs equal the failed plan's request. While it waits, every member row is off with one reason and no member's path is read, and a click (which checks again, `member_game_path`) stores nothing. The reason says which wait it is (controller ruling 3b-F25): "Planning: pick a file once the plan has finished" while a plan or a scan-database read runs, and "The plan must run again first: pick a file once a new plan has finished" when none runs but the plan is out of date or failed (a `slotN` edit that changes the borrowed normal plans nothing, so the inputs differ with no plan running). The rows read `pick_waits_cached` (controller ruling 3b-F24): a running plan or read is checked live, and the rest is computed once per `PickKey` (the views key, the plan generation, `sdc.toml`'s revision, `evidence_rev`, `mesh_rev` and `plan_failed`), so a menu kept open on frames where none of that changes builds no export inputs; the click's check in `member_game_path` is never cached. Export also plans again first when a source changed on disk (`check_sources`); a pick does not look at the files. The path comes from the settled job's output alone (`member_game_path_in`), never from an explicit path a pending edit asks for, so a pick never stores a path the producer does not write and an output with no game location keeps its error. The index never holds a prediction either, and `view_reads` holds each job's output, so a link resolved before a plan lands is resolved again when the output moves (controller ruling 3b-F16; `slot_links::a_link_is_recomputed_when_the_plan_moves_its_output_back`). A path is never composed from a source folder and an output name, and `slot_path_key` is applied on the lookup side only. The index finds the set that writes an output by membership (it walks each set's members), and the path function reads only the two folders and the item's settled output (`member_game_path_in(roots, item)`), so a packed output whose set borrows its slot, or one mapped to another folder, keeps its key. An output under no `textures` folder (a legacy `output_name`) has no game location: it is in no entry, and From another set disables it (`NO_GAME_PATH`). The member list and the index read the folders through `sets::output_roots` (one read of the export configuration and one of the export folder, over `AppModel::output_root_in`), once per menu call or index build, never per member; the export inputs read them once per pass, not per item (`export_rel_in`, `export_key_in`). The read-count test pins a warm menu call with members at four reads of each with an Also tick on CS (two views lookups, the rows' cache key and `output_roots`), the same with more members and items; while a plan is out the list adds no read beyond the views lookups and builds no export inputs (controller rulings 3b-F6, 3b-F11, 3b-F14, 3b-F21 and 3b-F24, which withdraws 3b-F21's once-per-call bound; `slot_links::the_output_index_holds_where_each_file_is_written`, ten layouts through a real export, checked again with the consumer exported; `slot_links::the_slot_menu_builds_the_export_inputs_once_per_call`, `…::the_menu_and_the_index_read_the_folders_once_whatever_the_members`, `…::a_member_pick_waits_for_the_plan_and_then_stores_the_written_path`, `…::a_removed_mapping_offers_no_old_path_before_or_after_the_plan`, `…::a_member_pick_after_a_failed_replan_is_refused`, `…::an_open_slot_menu_builds_the_settled_check_once`, `…::the_settled_check_is_built_again_after_each_change_of_its_key`, `…::the_click_checks_the_plan_again_without_the_rows_cache`, `txst_map::with_the_plan_out_of_date_and_none_running_a_member_pick_says_it_must_plan_again`, `slot_menu::the_game_path_follows_the_current_prefix_not_the_last_plan`).
  - **One answer to "does this set write an entry".** `pbr_export::writes_entry` is `exports_json` (CS, a JSON set, a match that applies) and the statics rule keeping the entry (`pgp_entry_wanted`; a CS set with statics set to Never writes none). The link display, the feature defaults (`features`), the missing-map suppression and the cleared state read it through `link_slots`, and `written_json_entries` filters by it. On a set that writes no entry a stored link or clear changes no feature default and drops no missing-map row, and an own file under `slotN = ""` shows as itself (no dim chip, no "Left out of the JSON" sentence): `slot_shown`'s `Cleared` needs the file left out of `features`. `link_slots` asks `writes_entry` only of a set with a `slotN` string. The one exception is a record fill (controller ruling 3b-F13, withdrawing 3b-F9's record-fill clause): a stored `slotN` of any value keeps a TXST record from filling that slot (Note HG) on every set, a set that writes no entry included, as on main, because that decision feeds the engine's `borrowed_normal` input and so the roughness bake, and encoded bytes do not change. So on a statics-Never mapped set a typed path or a hand-edited `slotN` over a record-provided fill hides that fill in the editor, as on main (`slot_links::a_stored_slot_command_keeps_a_record_fill_out_as_on_main`, `txst_map::a_stored_slot_key_keeps_the_record_normal_out_of_the_engine_inputs`). Readers that promise the written result read `writes_entry` too: the no-albedo Info row; the 3D preview's candidate entry leaves the command out (JR7).
  - **Validation.** Export's blocks read what the JSON names (`sets::json_files`, over the fields the feature keys read: `feature_slots` and `linked`), in three states: a local file (an own or borrowed fill no `slotN` overrides, or a link that names a set's output, on the row it fills), a typed path no set writes (it fills its game slot for the JSON and is never missing), or nothing (a cleared slot). So "{feature} is on but the set has no …" counts a linked or typed-path height, coat normal, fuzz, glow or subsurface and does not count a cleared one; "_cnr and _f both use slot 7" and "_g and _sk both use slot 3" need two own or borrowed files the JSON keeps, so a `slotN` on that slot (a link, a typed path or a clear) clears the clash; and the "has no output path" check skips a slot whose `slotN` the JSON writes, a clear included (`slots_of` writes the string). One helper answers "does the JSON keep this fill" for the clash, the output-path check and the editor's conflict line with its Remove buttons (`sets::json_keeps_fill`, read by `view_row` without allocating), so on every set the editor shows a conflict only where the JSON keeps both fills: on a set that writes an entry, in the run or not, that is its effective slots (a `slotN` on the slot leaves no conflict); on a set that writes none (its statics drop it), its own and borrowed fills. Export checks a set in the run in its blocks, and a set left out of the run only in `prepare` and only when it has a stored slot command (its entry is held with the reason); a set left out with none is not checked (controller rulings 3b-F8, 3b-F12 and 3b-F17; `set_editor_ui::a_slot_the_json_overrides_shows_no_conflict_as_export_has_none`, `slot_links::export_takes_a_typed_path_over_a_clashing_slot_7_or_3`). On a set that writes no entry these read its own and borrowed fills, as before. The typed-path state changes a block for a set whose `slotN` names no set's output: Parallax on with a typed slot-4 path no set writes no longer blocks, and a typed slot-7 path over an own `_cnr` and `_f` no longer clashes.
  - **Dependencies and scope.** A link that names a set's output is a dependency exactly as a borrow is; scope and dependencies come from the link as `link_slots` resolved it, so a `slotN` string that names no settled output (a typed path, or a path the producer no longer writes) is an external path with no host and no dependency (controller ruling 3b-F5). Export set, Export group and Export N sets bring the link's set in as they bring a borrow's host (`with_hosts`, under the same excluded-group rule); they read the links of the plan on screen before they wait for a pending plan, so a producer only the landing plan names stays out of the scope, and the consumer's entry is held with "not exported yet" when that producer's file is not on disk (a file already there satisfies the dependency) (controller ruling 3b-F20, parked for the final fix wave); the finalizer's dependencies of a JSON entry are the local files `json_files` names (`pbr_export::prepare`), so a linked file is checked like a borrowed one (written or up to date in this run, or on disk for a set left out on purpose, HF9) and the entry is held with the same note when it is not. An own or borrowed file that a `slotN` (a link, a typed path or a clear) overrides is not in the JSON, so it is no dependency of the entry; the set's own conversion jobs and a borrow's scope are unchanged. So a clear over a borrowed file never exported lets the entry in (`partial_export::a_clear_over_a_never_exported_borrow_lets_the_entry_in`). An entry of a set left out of the run gates nothing (HF9). A set with a stored slot command is written only when its own checks pass (the clash, missing-texture, feature-clash, prefix, slot-path and output-path blocks, `pbr_export::set_blocks`); otherwise it is held with "not in JSON: {set} ({reason})" and the run goes on. A set with none is not checked, as on main: its files on disk are accepted and its entry is written (controller rulings 3b-F7 and 3b-F12; `partial_export::an_entry_left_out_of_the_run_is_held_when_its_own_checks_refuse_it`, `…::with_no_slot_command_an_excluded_set_is_written_as_main_writes_it`). Tests `partial_export::a_scoped_export_treats_a_linked_lender_as_a_borrowed_one`, `an_excluded_linked_lender_holds_the_entry_as_a_borrowed_one_does` and `a_borrow_under_a_link_is_no_dependency`.
  - **The no-albedo Info.** Decided from the JSON the writer produces: an explicit `slot1` (a link, a typed path or a clear) is what the JSON names for slot 1, so the mesh keeps nothing and the "No albedo in this set; the mesh keeps {path}…" row (HL6) is not raised; `pbr_export::no_albedo_slot1` is `None` then, and `slots_of` writes the same JSON as before (the explicit `slot1` won already). Test `slot_links::with_a_linked_slot_1_the_no_albedo_info_is_not_raised`.

## Note HH — Sets share a helper (main team, 2026-11-09)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JW5, 2026-11-20)
- **HH5 Dropping a helper on a set shares it (owner's rulings; agent ruling for the code, 2026-11-09).** A shareable helper is an `ItemKind::Helper` with a `HelperRole::Raw` role. `drop_choice(Share)` on one writes `share = ["<key>"]` in the target's `sdc-project.toml` entry (`SetsDoc::add_share`, one undo step "Share <name> with <set>", no file, no `occupant` check) and refuses a target with no member with "<set> has no texture of its own; move one in first"; on its own set it does nothing, and neither does a no-set helper on the set its name puts it in (`dir/base`, where **Back to its automatic set** returns it). A no-set helper dropped on a set asks (`AskChoice { from: "no set" }`, owner), and its dialog leaves Duplicate out; every other no-set texture still moves (HF1). The dialog's Share reads "Share (use this file here too, no copy)" for a helper, and a finished texture still borrows (`borrow.slotN`). The Textures column's helper row is a drag source (`Sense::click_and_drag`, payload `DragItem`), so a no-set helper can reach a card; a click still selects. `engine_inputs` emits `SetEdit::Share` after the `NoSet` edits. Test `crates/gui/tests/helper_share.rs`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note JW3, 2026-11-20)
- **HH7 What the views show (agent ruling, 2026-11-09; final review 2026-11-09).** `SetView.shared_helpers` holds the item keys of the set's `share` lines that match an item (`path_key_matches`: any case, or without the extension) and are not the set's own members, which the engine ignores (a line matching no item is a `Missing::File`, as `add` is). The set editor draws one row per shared helper under the slot rows, outlined amber, named `<file> (shared)` so the user sees why it is not a slot (controller ruling); a click anywhere on the row selects the helper, and its ✕ ("Stop sharing <file>", `AppModel::stop_sharing`) removes every line that matches the file the same way. Only the ✕ is off on a read-only document, with the reason on hover. `stop_sharing` refuses a file the set does not share ("<set> does not share <file>", no undo step) and a read-only document. The row keeps clear of the editor's floating scroll bar. The helper panel lists every set in `shared_with`, used or not, under "Shared with" as links to those sets. `channel_helpers` offers a helper whose `shared_with` holds the set, and a pick writes the file's one `[files]` row, so it applies wherever the file is used. `HelperReport.used_by` (engine) holds the sharing sets whose jobs it lands in; `items::used_by` gives their base names when its own set fills nothing, and then `target_line` and the Textures row read `used by <names>`. A helper shared but used by no set still reads "not used: <reason>".
